do

local function initializeAll()
    -- task.wait(0.3) -- Removido para execução instantânea
    if State.nightModeEnabled then
        pcall(applyTheme)
    end
    if State.antiLagEnabled then enableAntiLag() end
    if State.fovEnabled then pcall(_G.MiseryEnableCustomFov) end
    if State.unwalkEnabled then startUnwalk() end
    if State.infJumpEnabled then startInfJump() end
    if State.removeAccEnabled then
        for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p.Character then for _, obj in ipairs(p.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Accessory") or obj:IsA("Hat") then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
            end end
        end
    end

    local function sync(ref, val) if ref then ref(val, true) end end
    sync(toggleRefs.autoSteal, State.autoStealEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.antiRagdoll, State.antiRagdollEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.antiDie, State.antiDieEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.infJump, State.infJumpEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.medusaCounter, State.medusaCounterEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.antiMedusa, State.antiMedusaEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.aimbot, State.batAimbotToggled)
    sync(toggleRefs.autoLeft, State.autoLeftEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.autoRight, State.autoRightEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.batCounter, State.batCounterEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.unwalk, State.unwalkEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.fpsBoost, State.fpsBoostEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.antiLag, State.antiLagEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.uiLocked, State.uiLocked)
    sync(toggleRefs.fov, State.fovEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.nightMode, State.nightModeEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.removeAcc, State.removeAccEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.noCollide, State.noCollideEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.autoGrabStop, Steal.AutoGrabStopEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.tpBat, State.tpBatEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.visionBat, State.visionBatToggled)
    sync(toggleRefs.intro, INTRO.enabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.matchMusic, INTRO.matchEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.headless, State.headlessEnabled)
    sync(toggleRefs.korblox, State.korbloxEnabled)
    if State.laggerOpen then
        task.defer(function()
            if _G.MiseryOpenLagger then _G.MiseryOpenLagger(true) end
        end)
    end
    if _G.MiseryAnimationPack then
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.15)
            _G.MiseryAnimationPack.Apply(State.animationPack or "OFF")
        end)
    end
    -- sync(toggleRefs.customFont, State.customFontEnabled)
    -- Now that UI is ready, enable custom font if needed (without blocking)
    -- if State.customFontEnabled then enableCustomFont() end

    SPD.refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    
    -- Initialize keybind buttons
    for keyRef, kbBtn in pairs(keybindBtnRefs) do 
        if Keys[keyRef] then 
            kbBtn.Text = getKeyName(Keys[keyRef])
            kbBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.panel
            kbBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
            kbBtn.TextColor3 = C.text
        end 
    end
    
    -- Sync Night Hub speed UI
    SPD.refreshSpeedModeLabel()

end

task.spawn(initializeAll)

mainOuter.Size = UDim2.new(0, WIN_W, 0, WIN_H)
mainOuter.Visible = State.guiVisible ~= false
miniBtn.Visible = State.guiVisible == false

StealProgressBar = {
    pbFrame = nil,
    progressPct = nil,
    progressFill = nil,
    dragger = {
        isDragging = false,
        dragStartPos = nil,
        startPos = nil
    }

-- Função para criar a barra de progresso
local function createStealProgressBar()
    local mkCorner = function(obj, r)
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner", obj)
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r)
        return corner
    end

    -- ===================== AUTO GRAB BAR (visual do script de referência) =====================
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame = Instance.new("Frame", gui)
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Name = "StealBar"
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 270, 0, 54)
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Position = _G.MiseryConfigToPosition(State.autoGrabPosition, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -10))
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(7, 7, 9)
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.03
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Active = true
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Visible = State.autoStealEnabled
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.ZIndex = 10
    mkCorner(StealProgressBar.pbFrame, 14)
    local pbStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", StealProgressBar.pbFrame)
    pbStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(125, 125, 132)
    pbStroke.Thickness = 1
    pbStroke.Transparency = 0.42

    -- Percent (top-left)
    StealProgressBar.progressPct = Instance.new("TextLabel", StealProgressBar.pbFrame)
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.Size = UDim2.new(0, 75, 0, 17)
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 4)
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.Text = "0%"
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 246)
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.TextSize = 13
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.28
    StealProgressBar.progressPct.ZIndex = 14
    progressPct = StealProgressBar.progressPct -- compatibilidade

    -- Radius (top-right)
    local progressRadLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", StealProgressBar.pbFrame)
    progressRadLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 17)
    progressRadLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -132, 0, 4)
    progressRadLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    progressRadLbl.Text = "Radius: " .. tostring(Steal.StealRadius)
    progressRadLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 246)
    progressRadLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    progressRadLbl.TextSize = 13
    progressRadLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    progressRadLbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    progressRadLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.28
    progressRadLbl.ZIndex = 14

    -- FPS / PING (segunda linha)
    local progressStats = Instance.new("TextLabel", StealProgressBar.pbFrame)
    progressStats.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 14)
    progressStats.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 22)
    progressStats.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    progressStats.Text = "FPS: -- | PING: --ms"
    progressStats.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(135, 135, 143)
    progressStats.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    progressStats.TextSize = 11
    progressStats.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    progressStats.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    progressStats.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.38
    progressStats.ZIndex = 14

    -- Track (pílula, na base)
    local fillRegion = Instance.new("Frame", StealProgressBar.pbFrame)
    fillRegion.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 9)
    fillRegion.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 39)
    fillRegion.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 21)
    fillRegion.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
    fillRegion.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fillRegion.ClipsDescendants = true
    fillRegion.ZIndex = 11
    mkCorner(fillRegion, 5)
    local fillStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", fillRegion)
    fillStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(75, 75, 82)
    fillStroke.Thickness = 1
    fillStroke.Transparency = 0.48

    -- Fill (prata com gradiente)
    StealProgressBar.progressFill = Instance.new("Frame", fillRegion)
    StealProgressBar.progressFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    StealProgressBar.progressFill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    StealProgressBar.progressFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
    StealProgressBar.progressFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    StealProgressBar.progressFill.ZIndex = 12
    mkCorner(StealProgressBar.progressFill, 5)
    local fillGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", StealProgressBar.progressFill)
    fillGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 250), Color3.fromRGB(105, 105, 115))
    fillGradient.Rotation = 90
    progressFill = StealProgressBar.progressFill -- compatibilidade

    -- Arrastável
    StealProgressBar.pbFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            StealProgressBar.dragger.isDragging = true
            StealProgressBar.dragger.dragStartPos = inp.Position
            StealProgressBar.dragger.startPos = StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Position
        end
    end)

    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
        if not StealProgressBar.dragger.isDragging then return end
        if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local delta = inp.Position - StealProgressBar.dragger.dragStartPos
        StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
            StealProgressBar.dragger.startPos.X.Scale, 
            StealProgressBar.dragger.startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, 
            StealProgressBar.dragger.startPos.Y.Scale, 
            StealProgressBar.dragger.startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
        )
        State.autoGrabPosition = _G.MiseryPositionToConfig(StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Position)
    end)

    UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local wasDragging = StealProgressBar.dragger.isDragging
            StealProgressBar.dragger.isDragging = false
            if wasDragging then
                State.autoGrabPosition = _G.MiseryPositionToConfig(StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Position)
                task.spawn(saveConfig)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- FPS medido + PING + Radius
    task.spawn(function()
        local statsService = game:GetService("Stats")
        local measuredFps = 0
        local frameCount = 0
        local frameTime = 0
        local fpsConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
            if not (StealProgressBar.pbFrame and StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Parent and StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Visible) then return end
            frameCount = frameCount + 1
            frameTime = frameTime + deltaTime
            if frameTime >= 0.5 then
                measuredFps = math.floor(frameCount / frameTime + 0.5)
                frameCount = 0
                frameTime = 0
            end
        end)
        local lastStatsUpdate = 0
        while StealProgressBar.pbFrame and StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Parent do
            local waitDuration = StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Visible and 0.05 or 0.2
            task.wait(waitDuration)
            if StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Visible and tick() - lastStatsUpdate >= 0.5 then
                lastStatsUpdate = tick()
                local ping = 0
                pcall(function()
                    local pingItem = statsService.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]
                    if pingItem then
                        local rawPing = pingItem:GetValue()
                        ping = math.floor((tonumber(rawPing) or 0) + 0.5)
                    end
                end)
                if progressStats then
                    progressStats.Text = "FPS: " .. tostring(measuredFps) .. " | PING: " .. tostring(ping) .. "ms"
                end
                if progressRadLbl then
                    progressRadLbl.Text = "Radius: " .. tostring(Steal.StealRadius)
                end
            end
        end
        if fpsConnection then fpsConnection:Disconnect() end
    end)

    -- Visibilidade conforme o auto steal
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.02) do
            if State.autoStealEnabled then
                StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Visible = true
            else
                StealProgressBar.pbFrame.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end)
end

createStealProgressBar()
applyMiseryProgressBarScale()

-- Auto save completo: estados, keybinds, escalas e todas as posições.
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(5) do pcall(saveConfig) end
end)
trackConn(LP.OnTeleport:Connect(function() pcall(saveConfig) end))

pcall(function()
    updateThemeUI()
    applyTheme()
    if State.fovEnabled then pcall(_G.MiseryEnableCustomFov) end
end)

print("[MISERY DUELS] Loaded! CTRL to hide/show.")
end

_G.MiseryTpBatV2 = _G.MiseryTpBatV2 or {hitting = false}

function _G.MiseryTpBatV2Swing()
    if _G.MiseryTpBatV2.hitting then return end
    _G.MiseryTpBatV2.hitting = true
    pcall(function()
        local char = LP.Character
        local bat = char and char:FindFirstChild("Bat")
        if not bat then
            local bp = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            bat = bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Bat")
            if bat and char then
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
            end
        end
        if bat then
            bat:Activate()
            local ev = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            if ev then ev:FireServer() end
        end
    end)
    task.delay(0.08, function() _G.MiseryTpBatV2.hitting = false end)
end

function _G.MiseryTpBatV2Tick()
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end
    local closest, best = nil, math.huge
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LP and player.Character then
            local tr = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tr and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local d = (root.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude
                if d < best then best = d; closest = player end
            end
        end
    end
    if not (closest and closest.Character) then return end
    local tr = closest.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not tr then return end
    if sethiddenproperty then
        pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(root, "PhysicsRepRootPart", tr) end)
    end
    local targetPos = tr.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0)
    local voidY = -500
    pcall(function() voidY = (workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight or -500) + 80 end)
    local unsafe = (targetPos.Y <= voidY) or (tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y < -120)
    if (not unsafe) and (root.Position - targetPos).Magnitude > 8 then
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
    end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, tr.Position) end
    _G.MiseryTpBatV2Swing()
end

-- Vision Bat Aimbot Heartbeat
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not State.visionBatToggled then return end
    if State.tpBatMode == "v2" then
        _G.MiseryTpBatV2Tick()
        return
    end
    if not (h and hrp) then return end
    local target, dist = getClosestPlayer()
    if target and target.Character then
        local tr = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if tr then
            if sethiddenproperty then
                sethiddenproperty(hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", tr)
            end
            local targetPos = tr.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0)
            if (hrp.Position - targetPos).Magnitude > 8 then
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
            end
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, tr.Position)
            tryHitVisionBat()
        end
    end
end)
